No final all-fix runtime anchor or actual >=24h observation is started by this supplement.\n\n## Phase 1 — Census and resumption\n- **P-001** `done` Classify the complete start population and record explicit per-agent evidence and unknowns. — note: ← WI-10008152 completed (done)\n- **P-002** `done` Conditionally wake unjustified parked agents and verify pickup. blocked-by: P-001 — note: ← WI-10008153 completed (done)\n\n## Phase 2 — Root cause\n- **P-003** `done` Diagnose premature-parking cases, correct confirmed instruction/runtime defects with maintained tests or owned follow-ups, and deliver the verified audit. blocked-by: P-002 — note: ← WI-10008154 completed (done)\n\n\n## Phase 3 — Prevention (measured 2026-10-10, su-92de4154)\n\n- **P-004** `done` Codex stale-auth recovery. psu-pty-host detects a codex turn that ends with a provider-auth error ('access token could not be refreshed', 'sign in again', 401) and does an identity-preserving carry-respawn of the codex child, so the fresh process re-reads the shared ~/.codex/auth.json. Mirror the existing quota-recovery cap (EI-21618720801156173). Add a pre-inject check: respawn before injecting a wake if the child's last turn failed auth. Root-cause leg: determine whether codex can reload auth from disk after a rotated refresh token (codex source/docs) and remove the rotation hazard. Tests in the psu-pty-host suite. Measured 2026-10-10: 46 parked sessions; all codex children started before the shared token's 2026-10-08T00:24Z rotation; live control 0. importance: urgent — note: ← WI-10008155 completed (done)\n- **P-005** `done` Failed carry-respawn binding must recover, not strand. Extend WI-10007305 / WI-10002078 (do not fork). On respawn-carry-dropped or respawn-binding-unconfirmed (successor-native-id-missing): (a) recover the successor native id from the session's codex home newest rollout with bounded retries; (b) if still unbound after a bounded window, relaunch through the identity-preserving successor route; (c) never let later deliveries loop forever as duplicate-delivery-id 'pending'. Measured: 57 parked sessions went silent at this event vs 3/87 live; includes the original auditor su-c210fe1b (stranded ~13h after its own self-compaction). importance: urgent — note: ← WI-10008156 completed (done)\n- **P-006** `done` 'Delivered' must mean 'a turn started'. The wake ledger and the coord:wake/send/dispatch results report turn-start proof (native-turn-start-confirmed) separately from message persistence. A delivery with no turn start within a bounded window escalates to host recovery (P-004/P-005 items above) and is reported as not-picked-up. First verify the peer claim [peer:su-c210fe1b] that wake-executor.ts degradeToInboxOrDrop reports 'delivered' on inbox fallback. Measured: loop fires to stalled sessions are all status=delivered (inbox / psu-socket-inject / suppressed-redundant) with no turn. importance: high — note: WI-10008157 done 22:17:40Z: turn_proof ledger dimension (migration 1441) + pickup sweep + D-005 enqueue semantics; live-verified after 1441 applied; spec AUTO-BAR-R-6-P-006@2 PASS.\n- **P-007** `done` stalled-loops-guard repairs instead of disarming. On a turns-stalled verdict, read host health (auth error, unbound respawn, pending duplicate deliveries) and route to the recovery items; disarm only when the host is healthy and turns are genuinely not wanted. Never leave a claim-holding AUTO session with zero wake sources: fall back to a bounded await plus a fleet-leader/owner alert. Reuse silent-halt-reconcile.ts; coordinate with the WI-37546 false-positive reviver (it only revives after new tool activity, which a wedged session cannot produce). Measured: 61 turns-stalled disarms; 37 dead-auth; 57 now hold claims with no wake. importance: high blocked-by: P-004, P-005 — note: ← WI-10008158 completed (done)\n- **P-008** `done` Fix the instructions that teach premature stops. Sources: harness/routines/loop.ts DEFAULT_LOOP_WAKE_PROMPT_TEMPLATE (~lines 124/130/145/149/278), agent-tools/loop/end.ts guidance.when (~146), agent-launch-core.ts headless startup body (~1123), scheduler/fleet-scope-admission.ts (~2710-2734). Replace 'blocked externally -> loop:end' and 'pass acknowledgeWakeLessAutonomy in the FIRST call' with: keep doing independent work; when everything left is blocked, record work_items:set_blocker plus a bounded events:await on the specific unblocking event (owner answer, work-item:unblocked, gate verdict) with on_timeout:'wake', then end the turn; loop:end only when done, released/handed off, or the owner said stop. Follow the su-persona-render-and-edit-path runbook; update loop.test.ts / end.test.ts / agent-launch-core.test.ts; run the su llm-test. importance: high — note: ← WI-10008159 completed (done)\n- **P-009** `done` Make the wake-less guard real. loop:end refuses an AUTO session that still holds unfinished claims and has no non-inbox wake source, unless the goal is done / claims released, an owner stop directive exists, or a typed fleet wind-down cue applies. The refusal's recovery args offer a bounded events:await template keyed to the stated blocker instead of acknowledgeWakeLessAutonomy:true. Tests in end.test.ts. Measured: 100 of 140 self-stopped parkers passed the bypass flag; 51 are AUTO + holding claims + no wake source. importance: high — note: ← WI-10008160 completed (done)\n- **P-010** `done` Blocked claims stop consuming the scheduler concurrency cap. A claim with a recorded external blocker (work_items:set_blocker) no longer counts against the 2-claim cap (or auto-parks on the blocker with a resume watch), so an agent holding two blocked items can pull independent work instead of parking. Measured: 16 parked agents cite the 2/2 claim cap. — note: WI-10008161 closed done: adequacy pass, mutation caught (21025441), certify card EI-25650609330847283.\n- **P-011** `done` Detector and honest liveness. (a) The presence oracle reports a session that cannot take a turn (dead auth, unbound respawn, quota/403/model refusal) as wedged/suspect, not 'parked'. (b) A standing alarm (existing condition-reconciler / infra-liveness surface) fires when wedged sessions, or parked AUTO sessions holding claims with no wake source, exceed a threshold, with per-class counts visible on /admin. (c) Record why nothing alarmed while 61 loops stalled for days. importance: high — note: ← WI-10008162 completed (done)\n- **P-012** `done` One-time remediation of the current population (after the auth and binding recovery items land, or by manual identity-preserving respawn if the owner approves sooner): respawn about 59 cannot-think and 57 binding-stranded sessions, preserving each one's model/account/carry; re-wake AUTO bypassers with a corrected brief; preserve legitimate owner/budget/dependency waits; verify pickup by new tool activity, never by 'queued'. importance: high blocked-by: P-004, P-005 — note: ← WI-10008163 completed (done)\n- **P-013** `blocked` Re-measure and close. 24h after the fixes, rerun the census with this audit's queries (scripted). Success: cannot-think = 0, binding-stranded at or below the live baseline, AUTO+claims+no-wake near 0. Publish the verified report and close WI-10008017. blocked-by: P-004, P-005, P-006, P-007, P-008, P-009, P-010, P-011, P-012 — note: ← WI-10008164 blocked (1 external blocker)\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n## Decisions\nThis is a new October owner-directed audit, independent of the terminal August and September repair scopes. Completed tasks, explicit owner pauses and verified blocking dependencies remain valid reasons to park.\n\n### D-001 — Owner approved the Phase 3 prevention plan; su-92de4154 builds it directly\nDate: 2026-10-10\n[owner:Avi 2026-10-10 ~16:15Z, AskUserQuestion answer in su-92de4154, directive #1583] Approved P-004..P-013 and chose route 'Me, directly' (this session, no fleet). Execution order: P-004 + P-005 first (same psu-pty-host surface, serial), then P-006..P-011, then P-012 and P-013. The draft-to-active activation audit is still owed before plans:start.\n\n### D-002 — Do NOT respawn the currently wedged agents before the durable fixes land\nDate: 2026-10-10\n[owner:Avi 2026-10-10 ~16:15Z, AskUserQuestion answer 'Wait for the fixes'] The ~116 wedged sessions (59 dead-auth/quota/403/model, 57 failed carry-respawn binding) stay parked until P-004/P-005 land; remediate them under P-012 afterwards. No ad-hoc mass respawn or wake sweep before then.\n\n### D-003 — P-005(a) recovers the successor id from the exact-child writer lock, not the newest rollout; window anchored to child spawn\nDate: 2026-10-10\n[self-imposed, su-92de4154 2026-10-10 ~18:25Z; measured] P-005(a)'s text says 'recover the successor native id from the session's codex home newest rollout'. Measured on live homes: Codex creates the rollout file LAZILY at the first turn (healthy respawns: thread-writer lock born 0.5-2s after spawn, rollout born 2-50s later, after the carry write). At binding time the newest rollout in the home is therefore the PREDECESSOR's thread, so a newest-rollout resolver would bind the wrong identity. Decision: (a) keeps the existing exact-child oracle (codexNativeIdForPty: thread-writer lock held by this PTY's native pid) as the only identity source, and the bounded retries are its polling within a window anchored to the child's SPAWN (default 120s, env PAPERCUSP_PSU_PTY_UNBOUND_CHILD_WINDOW_MS), not a fresh 600s per attempt. Window basis: lock birth after respawn on 103 confirmed carry bindings since 2026-10-07: p50 1.1s, p90 2.8s, p99 5.6s (one lock-reuse outlier). The p99 545s confirmation tail is the operator re-anchor report, so only successor-native-id-missing (never successor-reanchor-unconfirmed) triggers (b). (b) relaunches through recycleChild in carry-respawn mode, re-delivering the stashed undelivered carry, bounded (default 2, env PAPERCUSP_PSU_PTY_UNBOUND_RELAUNCH_MAX), skipped when the owner typed into the child. (c) after the budget is spent, turn/reset deliveries are refused at admission with 'successor-unbindable' (re-probing the oracle each time, so a child that later binds self-heals) instead of being ACKed into an endless pending-delivery-id loop.\n\n### D-004 — P-006 turn-start proof is a separate ledger dimension resolved from real-time signals only; window 600s\nDate: 2026-10-10\n[self-imposed, su-92de4154 2026-10-10 ~19:35Z; measured] Verified the peer claim: wake-executor.ts degradeToInboxOrDrop returns {kind:'delivered', channel:'inbox'}; inbox, pty-inject, resume*, cold-fresh-successor and unconfirmed psu injects are all booked status='delivered' with no turn proof. Measured 24h: inbox 21,970 delivered / 13,131 with no tool_use within 10m; psu-socket-inject 1,119 / 259; resume-headless 1,943 / 222; resume-rehome-backend 6 / 6.\n\nDesign:\n(1) status='delivered' keeps its meaning (handed to a channel / persisted). A NEW column turn_proof carries the turn: host-attested (psu host replied 'delivered' under requireNativeTurnStart, i.e. its native-turn-start-confirmed event; or quota-recovery pickup confirmed) | observed (a real-time turn signal after delivered_at) | awaiting (no proof yet, before pickup_deadline_at) | not-picked-up (deadline passed with no signal) | superseded (coalesced / suppressed-redundant: content rides another delivery). Plus turn_started_at, pickup_deadline_at, pickup_escalated_at.\n(2) Evidence sources are REAL-TIME only: tool_invocations by the subscriber after delivered_at, and the psu host's own execution.turnStartedAt / native-turn-start-confirmed event. The transcript store is excluded: measured ingestion lag p50 434s, p90 1241s, so it would mark live pickups as missed.\n(3) Window default 600s (env PAPERCUSP_WAKE_PICKUP_WINDOW_SEC). Basis: delivery-to-first-tool-use p95 is 200s (resume-headless), 834s (inbox), 1006s (psu-socket-inject); 600s covers resume p95 and psu p90 (160s).\n(4) not-picked-up emits wake:not-picked-up:<subscriberId>. Host-recovery escalation is per delivery and bounded to ONE re-queue, only when the subscriber's psu host reports a condition P-004/P-005 recover (auth/quota blocked, unbound successor). Inbox not-picked-up rows are reported, never mass re-woken (D-002); loop-level repair stays with P-007, alarms with P-011.\n(5) coord:wake/send/dispatch report pickup from this column: pickupConfirmed is true only when the queued deliveries are host-attested or observed.\n\n### D-005 — P-006 slice 3: pickup is reported by a LATER seam; the enqueue result keeps pickupConfirmed:false\nDate: 2026-10-10\n[self-imposed, su-92de4154 2026-10-10 ~20:05Z; amends D-004 (5)] D-004 (5) said coord:wake/send/dispatch set pickupConfirmed true when the queued deliveries are host-attested or observed. Verified against the code: those tools return as soon as the wake is ENQUEUED (wakeRecipients -> emitAwaitedEvent -> insertDeliveries; the pump runs afterwards via setImmediate), so at that seam no delivery can yet carry host-attested/observed. The doc-claim packages/operator-core/lib/doc-claims/wake-pickup-unconfirmable.ts (EI-22733985246154315) pins exactly this: pickupConfirmed is the literal false at the enqueue seam, and a real handshake must report LATER from a different seam. Decision: (1) the enqueue results keep pickupConfirmed:false; (2) they gain a pickup handle per woken target: the event key wake:not-picked-up:<target> (emitted by the slice-2 pickup sweep) and the pickup window, so a sender awaits failure instead of trusting 'queued'; (3) the later seam is the delivery read: listRecentDeliveries / events:status report turn_proof + turn_started_at per delivery. No positive 'picked-up' event is emitted: at ~20k turn-capable deliveries/day it would flood the key-fire latch; an expired window with no not-picked-up emit is the positive reading. Slice 2 (pickup sweep) is landed and tested; slice 3 follows this shape.\nRelated: P-006\n\n### D-006 — P-007 slice 2: silent-halt stops counting a session's own inbox watcher as a wake source; host-aware wake; fleet-leader alert; no at-disarm await\nDate: 2026-10-10\nDate: 2026-10-10\n[self-imposed, su-92de4154 2026-10-10 ~23:40Z; measured, evidence on WI-10008158 checkpoint]\n\nMEASURED. The real sweepSilentHalts({dryRun:true}) against the operator DB (control: port 5432, routine row present) checked 118 sessions, halted 3, exempted 115 — every exemption 'pending-await xN (a live wake source)' (silent-halt-reconcile.ts:228). SQL split: 51-53 AUTO claim-holding sessions with a disarmed loop hold ~87-132 work items and their ONLY pending await is their own `coord:inbox-wake:<self>` watcher, which fires only on an inbound message. Host classification of those 51 with the slice-1 reader+classifier: 44 healthy host (87 items; 22 idle >6h; 25 fleet members), 6 needs-relaunch:unbound-successor (36 items), 1 needs-relaunch:quota-blocked. silent-halt woke 29/14/2 sessions on 10-07/08/09 and none since 10-09 02:51Z.\n\nDECISION.\n(1) silent-halt-reconcile's candidate read excludes the subscriber's OWN inbox-wake await (key = inboxWakeKey(owner), COORD_INBOX_WAKE_PREFIX) from pending_awaits. Every other pending await still exempts (a named producer may legitimately be pending). The owner page text says so.\n(2) Host-aware wake leg: before the R1 wake, classify the host with readStalledLoopHost + classifyStalledLoopHost (slice 1). needs-relaunch => NO wake (a wake bounces off an unbound/walled child; keeps D-002's no-wake-sweep-of-wedged rule) and page immediately with the HOST UNHEALTHY why + remedy. healthy / recoverable-on-wake / no-host / read failure => wake as today (fail-open to current behaviour).\n(3) Fleet-leader alert: when the halted session is a fleet member and its owner page is NEWLY created (not deduped), also send to @fleet-leader:<slug> (resolved with audience.ts expandAudience, the coord:send resolver) and wake that leader. Rides the page's per-(owner, disarm-epoch) dedupe, so one alert per halt epoch.\n(4) Page cap counts only NEWLY created pages, so a standing deduped page (a still-halted session) can no longer starve new halts out of the 5-per-sweep cap.\n(5) NO bounded await armed at stalled-loops disarm. With (1), silent-halt is the bounded fallback (fires within max(30 min, 5 intervals) of the disarm). An await armed at disarm would itself count as a pending await and re-hide the session — the exact blind spot measured above.\n\nEFFECT ON DEPLOY: about 44 healthy-host parked AUTO sessions become visible at once; MAX_WAKES_PER_SWEEP=5 at the routine's cadence spreads their wakes over roughly 2-3 hours. D-002 is respected: P-004/P-005 are done, and wedged (needs-relaunch) hosts are paged, not woken; P-012 still owns their remediation.\n\nOUT OF SCOPE / follow-up: 23 AUTO sessions (~75 items) whose awaits are untimed bound event awaits (work-item:done/unblocked, conversation:answered) remain exempt; whether those producers progress needs its own measurement. P-007's 'pending duplicate deliveries' host input is slice 3.\nRelated: P-007, P-012\n\n### D-007 — P-007 slice 3: precise unbound-successor judge + pending-turn-stuck host condition, one shared tail judge for P-006 and P-007\nDate: 2026-10-10\nDate: 2026-10-10 [self-imposed, su-92de4154 2026-10-10 ~23:50Z; measured, evidence on WI-10008158 checkpoint]\n\nMEASURED (read-only probes over all ~262 live psu hosts, 6h window):\n(1) duplicate-delivery-id alone is routine host dedupe: 86/222 healthy hosts emit it. Not a wedge signal by itself.\n(2) The slice-1 unbound-successor judge OVER-REPORTS. It took the newest binding event KIND (respawn-binding-unconfirmed | respawn-carry-dropped) from the per-owner tail, ignoring reason, host generation, and later turns. Of 33 hosts it called unbound, only 6 carry reason successor-native-id-missing (the only reason the host itself treats as unbindable, psu-pty-host.mjs isUnbindableChild / retry predicate); 12 are 'superseded', 10 'successor-reanchor-unconfirmed', the rest mixed; 10 delivered a turn after the event; 4 events predate the current host process. 8 of 11 hosts the precise rule reclassifies made tool calls after the event, and 5 'superseded'/carry-dropped hosts run 95-245 tool calls/hour. Since slice 2 pages (not wakes) needs-relaunch sessions, the over-report would leave healthy sessions unwoken. The same judge drives the P-006 pickup sweep (hostRecoverableCondition), so both inherit it.\n(3) Pending duplicate deliveries ARE a real wedge signal when they persist: hosts holding >=2 deliveries as state:'pending' with the oldest >=30m and no turn-delivered since = 6 hosts; 5 already caught (incl. the 2 true P-005 wedges with ~180 pending re-sends over 70m), 1 classified healthy (no turn in 13h).\n(4) No host has ever emitted successor-unbindable-refused: the host's duplicate-delivery-id short-circuit runs BEFORE the P-005(c) refusal, so a delivery ACKed before the child became unbindable re-polls as pending-delivery-id forever.\n\nDECISION:\n(a) One shared pure tail judge in turn-proof.ts, used by BOTH readPickupHostView (P-006) and classifyStalledLoopHost (P-007); the two duplicated latest-binding scans go away. PickupHostView carries the verdicts (unboundSuccessor, stuckPending), not a raw event kind.\n(b) unbound-successor := within the CURRENT host process (event ts >= host startedAt), the newest of {respawn-binding-unconfirmed, respawn-binding-confirmed, respawn-carry-delivered, successor-unbindable-refused, turn-delivered} is successor-unbindable-refused, or respawn-binding-unconfirmed with reason successor-native-id-missing. respawn-carry-dropped and other unconfirmed reasons no longer imply unbound.\n(c) New PickupRecoverable 'pending-turn-stuck' := within the current host process, >=2 duplicate-delivery-id state:'pending' events, the oldest older than the pickup window (D-004, 600s default), no turn-delivered after it, and the host not visibly mid-turn (execution phase running with progress inside the window). Ordered after auth/quota/unbound. Never wake-recoverable: classifyStalledLoopHost -> needs-relaunch (remedy: identity-preserving relaunch, P-012); the pickup sweep reports it without the one re-queue, since a re-queued wake is ACKed into the same stalled pipeline.\n(d) (4) is recorded as a P-005(c) residue, not fixed here: the host-side ordering fix (refuse before the duplicate short-circuit, or expire pending ids of an unbindable child) is psu-pty-host work, filed separately.\n\n### D-008 — One wake-source predicate for silent-halt and P-009; dead work-item awaits never count (measured: none is a sole source today)\nDate: 2026-10-11\nDate: 2026-10-11 [self-imposed, su-92de4154 2026-10-11 ~00:40Z; measured, WI-10008290]\n\nMEASURED (read-only): the real sweepSilentHalts({dryRun:true}) against the operator DB (control line: port 5432, routine row present) checked 131 sessions, halted 39, exempted 68 for 'pending-await'. Probe: .papercusp/scratch/p007e-untimed-awaits.mts, outputs p007e-untimed-awaits{,2,3}.out. Each exempt owner was classified by its STRONGEST open await, ignoring the own coord:inbox-wake watcher (D-006):\n- timed await (expires_ts set): 18 owners, 28 claims\n- untimed work-item:claimable watch: 27 owners, 13 claims\n- untimed conversation:answered: 9 owners, 28 claims\n- untimed system events (fleet:*, plan:acceptance, goal:*, composed-root:*, release:*, escalation:*): 10 owners, 13 claims\n- untimed work-item:unblocked on an item the owner itself holds as blocked: 3 owners, 24 claims\n- ONLY dead work-item awaits: 0 owners.\nDead work-item awaits do exist but are never the sole source today: 11 awaits on work-item:done:<an item the subscriber itself holds> (self-deadlock, 5 owners), and 17 untimed awaits on items no one holds (done:open 7, unblocked:open 4, unblocked:blocked 3, done:blocked 3).\n\nDECISION. (1) There is ONE wake-source predicate, shared by silent-halt (evaluateSilentHalt's pendingAwaits) and P-009's loop:end guard. It is not re-derived per caller. It does NOT count: the session's own inbox watcher (D-006); a done-await on an item the subscriber holds; an await on a terminal or missing target. Today this flips 0 sessions; it prevents a false exemption by construction. (2) OPEN for P-009, deliberately not ruled here: whether an UNTIMED claimable/conversation/system-event await counts as a sole wake source for a claim-holding AUTO session. That is the unverified mass (46 owners, 54 claims). If P-009 requires a timed await there, its refusal's recovery template (a bounded events:await) already supplies one.\n\n### D-009 — P-009 rules D-008 (2): a claim-holding autonomous session needs a GUARANTEED wake source to end its loop; silent-halt keeps counting untimed live awaits\nDate: 2026-10-11\n[self-imposed, su-92de4154 2026-10-11 ~01:10Z; measured]\n\nMEASURED (read-only, 2026-10-11 ~00:50Z, open non-inbox awaits by family): work-item:claimable is an id-less backlog-interest watch (114 awaits / 110 owners, filtered by kind/plan; the last batch of fires was ~11:09Z, ~13h earlier) -- it says 'I am idle, wake me for new work', not 'wake me for the work I hold'. conversation:answered rows are auto-armed when a question is asked (bound_to conversation-question, 71 awaits / 55 owners); the sampled keys had 0 fires in 24h.\n\nDECISION.\n(1) loop:end, for an owner in an autonomy-implying mode that still holds non-terminal work-items: the stop proceeds only when a GUARANTEED wake source survives -- a deliberate park that is a wake source under the D-008 (1) predicate AND bounded with wake-on-timeout (awaitGuaranteesRewake, the same rule as the turn-end halt tripwire), or a pending deliberate wake delivery. Untimed claimable / conversation / system-event watches do NOT count: they make no promise to fire, and 'untimed watch + held claim + no loop' is the strand shape this plan measured. acknowledgeWakeLessAutonomy does NOT override this refusal. Exits: the goal is achieved (disposition 'achieved', through the acceptance gate), the claims are released, a verified owner stop directive (new arg ownerStopDirectiveId: exists, same workspace, belongs to the loop owner, hook-captured), or a typed fleet wind-down cue. The refusal's recovery is a bounded events:await keyed to the blocker a held item states (typed event blocker ref with its recheckAt as the bound, else work-item:unblocked:<id> for a blocked item; never work-item:done:<held id>), or the checkpoint+release path when no item states a blocker.\n(2) silent-halt keeps counting untimed LIVE awaits as exemptions (policy any-live). It is a retroactive detector; making it stricter would page the unverified 46-owner mass at once, and that is P-011's call with its own measurement. The two callers share the D-008 (1) predicate for what is DEAD and differ only in this one strictness.\nCode: packages/operator-core/lib/events/await/wake-source-predicate.ts (shared), packages/operator-core/lib/agent-tools/loop/end.ts (guard). Tracked on WI-10008160.\n\n### D-010 — P-010: a hold parked on a typed external blocker stops counting against maxConcurrentClaims (narrows EI-11796)\nDate: 2026-10-11\nDate: 2026-10-11 [self-imposed, su-92de4154 ~02:30Z; measured]\n\nMEASURED (read-only, all tenants, 02:29Z): 188 claim holders; 56 hold >=1 item that is state blocked/needs-human WITH >=1 active external blocker (78 such items, max 5 on one holder). 113 holders are at >=2 held claims; 18 of them are at a 2-claim cap ONLY because of those holds (24 at a 1-claim cap). Matches the census's 16 parked agents citing 2/2.\n\nDECISION. The one concurrency oracle (claim-spec-store getActiveClaimsForBee / readClaimConcurrencyBatch -> evaluateClaimConcurrency) excludes a held row from activeClaims when BOTH hold: (1) status is blocked or needs-human, and (2) activeExternalBlockers(payload) is non-empty. Classification runs in JS with the real external-blockers parser over the selected externalBlockers array, so SQL cannot drift from the parser. The verdict gains blockerParkedIds so every surface still names those holds; they are not hidden. work_items:set_blocker is the lever: recording a typed blocker on a held item frees its slot without releasing it.\n\nWHAT STILL COUNTS (EI-11796's protection kept): a wip item even if it carries a blocker (the holder says it is working it); a plain claim-hold park with no typed blocker; an item blocked only by a work-item link (work_items:link rel:blocks) -- out of P-010 scope; resource-governor receipts stay excluded as before.\n\nWHY THIS NARROWS RATHER THAN BREAKS EI-11796. EI-11796 stopped a member from parking items without explanation and accumulating lanes. A typed external blocker is not an unexplained park: it names kind/capability/ref/evidence/next action, the hold registry re-checks it, and the reaper applies owner-ask defaults. Accumulation is self-limiting: when blockers clear, those items count again and the member cannot pull until it is back under its cap. No separate ceiling for parked holds; P-013's re-measure watches max parked-per-holder (5 today).\n\nSURFACES UPDATED WITH IT: get_next concurrencyBlockedRefusal text (drops 'declaring a blocker does not buy a slot', names set_blocker as the lever and lists blocker-parked ids), fleet:assignments capacity comment, loop/arm EI-20304485841686120 comment, P-008's HELD_BLOCKED_ITEM_STAYS_HELD advice. The workspace fact guard-rail:fleet-assignments-blocked-holds-count-for-claim-capacity is superseded when this lands.\n\n### D-011 — P-011(a): a wedged host makes the liveness oracle say suspect + wedged, not parked; default-on wherever the host is trusted as proof of life\nDate: 2026-10-11\nDate: 2026-10-11\nDate: 2026-10-11 [self-imposed, su-92de4154 ~03:20Z; code read, measurement pending on WI-10008162]\n\nREAD (code): liveness-oracle.ts psuHostPositiveAuthority RESCUES a subject to parked/recorded whenever findLiveHost returns a live host process (deriveVerdict, 'psuHostLive && psuHost.positive' branch). The host record and its lifecycle tail already carry the cannot-take-a-turn conditions (authBlocked, quotaBlocked, unbound successor, stuck pending deliveries) and one judge reads them (turn-proof.ts judgeHostTail / hostRecoverableCondition, D-007). The oracle never consulted it, so a dead-auth codex or an unbound successor read exactly like a healthy parked agent on every routing surface that trusts the host (recipient-liveness, inbox-wake, fleet:assignments, grading-audit and consult routing, owner-directive routing, gate-ownership).\n\nDECISION.\n(1) No new SessionState. A wedged session is 'suspect' (already non-actionable in classifyReadActionability, already confirmLiveness=true) and the verdict carries wedged: PickupRecoverable naming the condition. A new state would ripple through every consumer for no behavioral gain.\n(2) Eligible states: parked, draining, recorded. Never live (a live turn contradicts a stale host flag), never suspect/ended/null.\n(3) A post-pass in resolveSessionStates reads the host view through the SAME judge as the pickup sweep and stalled-loops guard (pickupHostView + hostRecoverableCondition over a 64 KB async tail), 16 at a time, only for non-cup subjects with a live host and an eligible verdict.\n(4) Default ON wherever psuHostPositiveAuthority is on (opts.hostCondition defaults to it): a caller that trusts a live host as proof of life must also hear when that host's child cannot think. Other callers opt in with hostCondition:true (the P-011(b) alarm will).\n(5) Absent wedged means not wedged OR not checked, never checked-healthy.\n\nMeasured: 2026-10-11 ~03:15Z, .papercusp/scratch/p011a-live-wedge.mts (read-only, real resolveSessionStates over listPresence, 414 subjects, psuHostPositiveAuthority on, leg OFF vs ON): 40 flips, all explained (28 quota-blocked, 8 pending-turn-stuck, 4 unbound-successor; 39 parked->suspect, 1 recorded->suspect); live 105 -> 105 untouched; cost 362 ms -> 562 ms for the whole roster. Flipped owners' last activity: 1 under 15 min (14 min), median ~5 h. psu-pty-host.mjs quota detector is level-based (observe(): not blocked -> reset(); published field = quotaBlockDetector.isBlocked()), so quota-blocked is current state, not sticky history. Tests: liveness-oracle-host-condition.test.ts 20/20 (test_runs 21027934) + 4 oracle suites 50/50 + 6 positive-authority caller suites 244/244; lint:tsc clean; behavioural-strands EXIT=0.\n\n### D-012 — P-011(b)+(c): one standing count alarm (agents-wedged / agents-stranded) on the coordination panel; why nothing alarmed while 61 loops stalled\nDate: 2026-10-11\nDate: 2026-10-11 [self-imposed, su-92de4154 ~03:40Z; measured unless tagged]\n\n(c) WHY NOTHING ALARMED. Four independent gaps, each now closed by an item of this plan:\n1. The repair path removed the evidence: stalled-loops-guard DISARMED a loop that stopped producing turns instead of repairing it, so the stalled population left the guard's own view (P-007) [per P-007 record].\n2. The retroactive detector was blind: silent-halt counted every session's own coord:inbox-wake watcher as a live wake source, so 115 of 118 checked sessions were exempt 'pending-await' and only 3 were judged halted (D-006, measured). After D-006 the same sweep judged 39 halted.\n3. Presence called a wedged host parked: the liveness oracle's psu-host positive authority rescued any session with a live host process to parked, so dead-auth / quota-walled / unbound sessions read as healthy idle and wakes to them reported woken:1 (D-011; 40 of 414 flipped once the host condition was judged).\n4. Nothing COUNTED the population [code read 2026-10-11]: EVALUATED_LIVENESS_SIGNATURES had no agent-population key; agent-productivity-watchdog only covers sessions that never produced an agent-origin call (never-booted / booted-never-worked), not a session that worked and then wedged; silent-halt pages one owner per halted session (capped at 5 a sweep). Per-victim remedies with no population count meant a correlated mass stall looked like N unrelated quiet sessions.\n\n(b) RULING. One census, no new panel or watchdog: packages/operator-core/lib/system-health/agent-strand-census.ts counts (i) WEDGED = the oracle's own wedged verdict (resolveSessionStates with psuHostPositiveAuthority + hostCondition explicitly ON) by class, and (ii) STRANDED = sweepSilentHalts({dryRun:true}).halted. It never re-derives liveness or wake sources. It rides the existing coordination panel (per-class metrics on /admin system health) and the existing infra-liveness alarm, signatures agents-wedged (>= 10) and agents-stranded (>= 5), severity blocker (pages once, deduped + flap-suppressed). The panel only WARNS: a crit would also raise the generic panel:coordination catch-all for the same condition. An unreadable leg, an errored panel, or a computed panel missing the census (older build's snapshot) is UNOBSERVED, never a recovery.\nStrictness: STRANDED uses D-008's wake-source predicate, NOT D-009's guaranteed-wake rule. An untimed watch on a live, reachable event is a real wake source; D-009's stricter bound governs only loop:end's refusal. Widening the alarm to 'no guaranteed wake' is not done here.\nThresholds: a single wedge is the pickup sweep's job and a single halt is silent-halt's; >= 10 wedged / >= 5 stranded means those remedies are not keeping up, usually behind one shared cause.\n\nMEASURED (live, operator DB control port 5432, .papercusp/scratch/p011b-strand-census.mts, 2026-10-11 ~03:35Z): checked 416, wedged 41 (quota-blocked 29, pending-turn-stuck 7, unbound-successor 5), stranded 32. Both alarms would fire today. Census cost ~1 s warm (13 s cold first call) inside the 30 s tick, in parallel with the panel's other reads.\n\n### D-013 — P-012: the quota-blocked class is a real wall on the shared default codex account; no respawn onto that route; host latch must expire at the stated reset\nDate: 2026-10-11\nDate: 2026-10-11\n[self-imposed, su-92de4154 ~04:15Z; measured unless tagged]\nMEASURED (read-only, .papercusp/scratch/p012-quota-walls.mts + p012-quota-hosts.mts): all 63 P-012 population owners run codex; 62 of 63 codex homes symlink auth.json to ~/.codex/auth.json, one ChatGPT account (id suffix 09ce2f). Its child transcripts report usage_limit_exceeded, 'try again at Oct 14th, 2026 8:24 PM', credits balance 0. 28 of the 29 quota-blocked hosts show that error; the newest is 03:32Z, so launches onto this account are still walling on their first turn. This retracts the 04:02Z WI-10008163 inference that these hosts were stale-blocked; the gateway accounts the burn signals reported as recovered are not the ones these sessions use.\nRULING. (1) P-012 does not respawn any codex session onto the default route while that account is walled; a respawn spends a launch and re-walls on the first turn. (2) Which route those sessions run on after this is the owner's call (account routing), asked on WI-10008163. (3) Durable defect, built regardless: the codex quota latch in psu-pty-host.mjs (makeQuotaBlockDetector) has no expiry and no corroborator, and the recycle recovery requires accountMode 'auto'. So a default-route codex host stays deadlocked after the provider's reset [inferred from code; a test will pin it]. The host must parse the stated reset ('try again at <date>' / 'try again in <duration>') into the latch, expire the latch at that time, and publish quotaResetAtMs on its record so liveness can say 'walled until X' instead of 'wedged'.\n\n### D-014 — Corrects D-013: the shared codex account recovered early (~04:25Z); P-012 relaunches on the same route are valid; the host latch also needs a probe\nDate: 2026-10-11\nDate: 2026-10-11\n[self-imposed, su-92de4154 ~04:35Z; measured unless tagged]\nD-013's premise that the 09ce2f account stays walled until Oct 14 is WRONG as of 04:25Z. A host relaunched then on the same ~/.codex/auth.json (unchanged since Oct 8) records has_credits=true and used_percent 1.0, with ~2M tokens of real work by 04:29Z. Capacity returned before the stated reset.\nRULING. (1) D-013 (1) is withdrawn: relaunching a latched codex host on its SAME route is the approved P-012 remediation (account preserved) and is effective now. (2) No owner routing decision is needed; the 04:08Z owner question was retracted by coord:send. (3) D-013 (3) stands and is extended: expiring the latch at the stated reset cannot see an early top-up, so a latched codex host must also probe. It lets one deferred wake through after a bounded, backed-off interval; a still-walled account repaints the banner and re-latches. (4) [inferred, not measured] host-code adoption after the WI-6554700010008412 edit is already relaunching hosts (31 started after 04:14Z; quota-blocked fell from 29 to 17). Re-measure before any manual relaunch.\n\n### D-015 — Acceptance BAR amendment\nDate: 2026-10-11\nAmendment-Id: amend-2298879d1875f645deeb1243\nRequest-Hash: 72d5d00950ff0c16570c47a430e3696ce9e0baec5a344ac49f027340bf547885\nActor: su-92de4154-4d25-4c00-b14f-2fee51639247\nReason: As-built correction for P-011 (WI-10008162), answering the objection in thread-post:1174377; approved at thread-post:1174447. R-11's seam is the host-condition judge in liveness-oracle.ts (applyHostConditionLeg / judgeHostCondition, judge in turn-proof.ts); it is not presence-wakeability.ts. Checks: liveness-oracle-host-condition.test.ts (oracle) + presence-snapshot-targeted.test.ts (coord:presence: dead-auth live host -> suspect/wedged auth-blocked; healthy control -> parked). Correction to the dry-run reason (see retraction thread-post:1174448): at review time the leg ran in resolveSessionStates and presence-snapshot.ts only; fleet:assignments (assignments.ts) and the fleet roster (fleet-roster.ts) were wired to the same judge afterwards (2026-10-11 ~15:49Z and ~16:00Z). Meaning of the bar unchanged (plan D-011).\nApproved-By: system:judge/su-f13c9cd7-2008-4ad1-8b15-2dc7da28c1be\nApproval-Ref: thread-post:1174447\nApproved-At: 2026-10-11T15:45:11.706Z\nRubric revision: 1 → 2\nSubject plan revision: 47 → 48\nChanged BARs: R-11\nPrior evidence, grades, vetting, author verdicts, and ship verdicts remain audit history and are revision-ineligible.\n\n### D-016 — Acceptance BAR amendment\nDate: 2026-10-11\nAmendment-Id: amend-c1f3e0320f027ea61e1af085\nRequest-Hash: 3cfb5be308d07f6302c4ee923aa3b06cf49cdb76d0085452d6d17c0a7e2b4dad\nActor: su-92de4154-4d25-4c00-b14f-2fee51639247\nReason: As-built widening for P-011 (WI-10008162), plan D-011. The one host-condition judge (judgeHostCondition / applyHostConditionLeg in liveness-oracle.ts) now runs on all four surfaces that trust a live psu host as proof of life: coord:presence (presence-snapshot.ts), fleet:assignments (agent-tools/fleet/assignments.ts), the fleet roster (fleet/fleet-roster.ts) and the oracle itself. Additive only: no prior scope or check file is removed; the bar's condition and falsifier are unchanged.\nApproved-By: system:judge/su-c03e29ab-bb35-42b2-9ee0-cfa249548fe0\nApproval-Ref: thread-post:1174520\nApproved-At: 2026-10-11T16:14:52.688Z\nRubric revision: 2 → 3\nSubject plan revision: 48 → 49\nChanged BARs: R-11\nPrior evidence, grades, vetting, author verdicts, and ship verdicts remain audit history and are revision-ineligible.\n\n### D-017 — Acceptance BAR amendment\nDate: 2026-10-11\nAmendment-Id: amend-57ed6cc20254fb609f5d8e7d\nRequest-Hash: f31d306921554afae1efee4378b992588b2c9e141f088dcb6a652f8430e7468d\nActor: su-92de4154-4d25-4c00-b14f-2fee51639247\nReason: As-built correction for P-011 (WI-10008162), plan D-012. As built, system-health/agent-strand-census.ts counts the liveness oracle's own wedged verdict by class, and system-health/liveness-alarm.ts raises signature 'agents-wedged' (threshold >= 10, severity blocker, deduped + flap-suppressed). If current source/evidence changes or emission refuses, reconcile that exact result instead of issuing another peer card.\nRelated: P-007, P-011, P-013\n\n### D-023 — P-013: silent-halt excludes blocker-only held work from the stranded-work predicate\nDate: 2026-10-12\nDate: 2026-10-12\n\nMeasured: WI-6554700010008462 records census #4 listing su-34e04dbe as stranded although its only held item WI-10007213 was needs-human with an active typed credential blocker. The current held_work_items subquery in silent-halt-reconcile.ts counts all nonterminal, non-observation holds without inspecting state or externalBlockers. D-010 already classifies the same legitimate park for claim concurrency.\n\nDecision: Extend D-010/P-010's exact predicate to silent-halt's held-work justification. A held row is excluded from this detector's actionable-work count only when status is blocked or needs-human AND the shared activeExternalBlockers parser finds at least one active typed external blocker. Reuse isParkedOnExternalBlocker; do not re-spell blocker semantics in SQL. When all held rows are blocker-parked, report a named exemption with the parked item IDs. When actionable and parked rows coexist, retain the halt verdict based on actionable rows and include the excluded IDs in the wake/page context. The blocker-held rows remain visible and still count again in the scheduler after their blockers clear.\n\nThis narrows only silent-halt's stranded-work predicate; it does not change D-010's concurrency oracle or its treatment of wip, unexplained claim holds, internal work-item dependencies, or resource-governor receipts.\nRelated: P-013\n\n### D-024 — Acceptance BAR amendment\nDate: 2026-10-16\nAmendment-Id: parked-fire97-method-coverage-424d5-v2\nRequest-Hash: 0987b7f30f072b36e8923a29daca0cf442efeb0442b855d4f37b6830e94b7fc4\nActor: su-424d5476-0b13-401a-a926-0aaa9f212391\nReason: Method-only Fire96 construction clarification. All fourteen original outcome fields, scopes, check declarations, fingerprints and pass semantics remain exactly unchanged; this does not claim atomic supplement coverage or runtime acceptance.\nApproval: not required — every change is additive ()\nRubric revision: 7 → 8\nSubject plan revision: 65 → 66\nChanged BARs: \nPrior evidence, grades, vetting, author verdicts, and ship verdicts remain audit history and are revision-ineligible.\nRequirements re-rendered from the amended criterion: R-4, R-5, R-6, R-7, R-8, R-9, R-11, R-12, R-13, R-14\n\n### D-025 — Acceptance BAR amendment\nDate: 2026-10-17\nAmendment-Id: parked-resume154-add18-preview-424d5-v1\nRequest-Hash: f306c6c67de3a0cc75ea6b9821a904c24cd858109ead6028b7c82ce804a5ea28\nActor: su-424d5476-0b13-401a-a926-0aaa9f212391\nReason: Add only18 independently reviewed construction outcomes R15..19/R22..34 from immutable Resume151 after A1..A7. Original14 complete criterion objects resubmitted exactly unchanged, original R12 terminal proof preserved. R20/R21 withheld. Construction targets explicitly unlocated; no bindings/grades/acceptance claimed.\nApproved-By: su-d5657ad4-21e4-470d-8e4f-ea552ca7d380\nApproval-Ref: thread-post:1188647\nApproved-At: 2026-10-17T17:48:49.753Z\nRubric revision: 8 → 9\nSubject plan revision: 67 → 68\nChanged BARs: R-15, R-16, R-17, R-18, R-19, R-22, R-23, R-24, R-25, R-26, R-27, R-28, R-29, R-30, R-31, R-32, R-33, R-34\nPrior evidence, grades, vetting, author verdicts, and ship verdicts remain audit history and are revision-ineligible.\n\n### D-026 — Silent-halt: a blocker park excuses a stop only when it names its wake; otherwise ONE wake, never a page (narrows D-010's silent-halt use)\nDate: 2026-10-21\nDate: 2026-10-21\n[self-imposed, su-cb4d4302, AUTO under owner directive #2094; implements report rpt_24408ef5-04d2-45ca-b56a-27ee3cca8c98 item #4; WI-6554700010018551]\n\nMEASURED (live, 2026-10-21 ~00:50Z): of 96 active blockers held by loop-off AUTO sessions with no actionable held work, 91 carry no recheckAt (runtime 45, gate 22, event 20, human 4). These are pre-default rows: updateExternalBlockerHistory stamps a default recheckAt on every set/re-declaration, but the blocker reaper selects on recheckAt <= now, which NULL never matches. So nothing revisits them and, with no await, nothing wakes the holder.\n\nDECISION. In evaluateSilentHalt, a blocker-parked hold keeps the 'holds-only-active-external-blockers' exemption only when every active blocker on it names a parseable re-check time (external-blockers.ts activeBlockersWithoutRecheck is the one predicate). A hold whose blocker names none falls through the usual clocks as a WAKE-ONLY halt: verdict.wakeOnly, listed in SilentHaltSweepResult.wakeOnly, woken once per disarm epoch with name-your-wake text (re-declare via work_items:set_blocker, which stamps recheckAt, or events:await with a timeout), queued after actionable halts under the per-sweep wake cap, and NEVER paged, never in the owner-facing alarm:silent-halt broadcast. They stay in result.halted, so the agent-strand census still counts them as stranded.\n\nUNCHANGED: D-010's claim-concurrency exclusion; D-009 (the detector still counts untimed live awaits; widening that remains P-011's call); actionable held work still pages after its wake.\n\nDRY RUN (real sweep, dryRun:true, 437 candidates): halted 238 = 180 pageable + 58 wake-only; the blocker exemption now spares 0 (previously those 58). The pageable count matches the live sweep's 179-180, so owner pages do not change.\n\n### D-027 — A confirmed wake miss on a warm-idle owner overrides positive psu-host authority (refines D-011)\nDate: 2026-10-21\nRuling (su-1622829b, 2026-10-21, AUTO, EI-26597499932248750). In `deriveVerdict`'s positive psu-host rescue (packages/operator-core/lib/agent-tools/coordination/liveness-oracle.ts), a live host no longer rescues an `ended`/`suspect` derivation to `parked`/`draining`/`recorded` when BOTH of these hold: `wakeAttemptMiss` is true (a turn-burning wake outlived WAKE_PICKUP_GRACE_MS with no activity after it) AND `warmIdle` is true (no turn for WARM_IDLE_ACTIVITY_MS). Such an owner now reads `suspect` + `wedged:'pending-turn-stuck'`, the same report the host-condition leg gives a host whose deliveries are stuck pending.\n\nWhy: measured on su-c5e6d0e3, a Codex host up 5.7 days whose child had sat at 0% CPU for 4.96 days. coord:presence and coord:send (positive authority) read it as parked and wakeable, and a send returned woken:1 with no recipient_dead. Every consumer without positive authority, including scorecards:emit's author-seat gate, read it as `ended`. D-011 already holds that a live host is not proof the session can think; this extends that from host-tail evidence to the oracle's own wake-ledger evidence.\n\nPrecision (D-007): either signal alone keeps the rescue. A miss alone (recent activity) is the ambiguity the rescue exists for, and warm-idle alone describes a legitimately parked owner. Both alone are pinned by unit cases in liveness-oracle-host-condition.test.ts.\n\nAlternative rejected: making every consumer pass identical authority options. That would leave the wedged host reading `parked` everywhere instead of `ended` everywhere, which hides the defect rather than fixing it.\n","raw":"---\ntitle: Owner-directed parked-agent census and conditional resumption\nslug: parked-agent-census-resumption-and-root-cause-2026-10-09\nstatus: ready\ncreated: 2026-10-10\nupdated: 2026-10-22\n---\n\n## Now\n\n**State:** ⚠ BAR READINESS BLOCKED (2026-10-21, after decision-added): bar_snapshot_proof_inadequate, bar_snapshot_grading_missing, bar_snapshot_proof_stale, bar_snapshot_evidence_plane_unmet, bar_snapshot_proof_missing, bar_snapshot_clause_not_accepted, bar_snapshot_vetting_stale. No final all-fix runtime anchor or actual >=24h observation is started by this supplement.\n\n## Phase 1 — Census and resumption\n- **P-001** `done` Classify the complete start population and record explicit per-agent evidence and unknowns. — note: ← WI-10008152 completed (done)\n- **P-002** `done` Conditionally wake unjustified parked agents and verify pickup. blocked-by: P-001 — note: ← WI-10008153 completed (done)\n\n## Phase 2 — Root cause\n- **P-003** `done` Diagnose premature-parking cases, correct confirmed instruction/runtime defects with maintained tests or owned follow-ups, and deliver the verified audit. blocked-by: P-002 — note: ← WI-10008154 completed (done)\n\n\n## Phase 3 — Prevention (measured 2026-10-10, su-92de4154)\n\n- **P-004** `done` Codex stale-auth recovery. psu-pty-host detects a codex turn that ends with a provider-auth error ('access token could not be refreshed', 'sign in again', 401) and does an identity-preserving carry-respawn of the codex child, so the fresh process re-reads the shared ~/.codex/auth.json. Mirror the existing quota-recovery cap (EI-21618720801156173). Add a pre-inject check: respawn before injecting a wake if the child's last turn failed auth. Root-cause leg: determine whether codex can reload auth from disk after a rotated refresh token (codex source/docs) and remove the rotation hazard. Tests in the psu-pty-host suite. Measured 2026-10-10: 46 parked sessions; all codex children started before the shared token's 2026-10-08T00:24Z rotation; live control 0. importance: urgent — note: ← WI-10008155 completed (done)\n- **P-005** `done` Failed carry-respawn binding must recover, not strand. Extend WI-10007305 / WI-10002078 (do not fork). On respawn-carry-dropped or respawn-binding-unconfirmed (successor-native-id-missing): (a) recover the successor native id from the session's codex home newest rollout with bounded retries; (b) if still unbound after a bounded window, relaunch through the identity-preserving successor route; (c) never let later deliveries loop forever as duplicate-delivery-id 'pending'. Measured: 57 parked sessions went silent at this event vs 3/87 live; includes the original auditor su-c210fe1b (stranded ~13h after its own self-compaction). importance: urgent — note: ← WI-10008156 completed (done)\n- **P-006** `done` 'Delivered' must mean 'a turn started'. The wake ledger and the coord:wake/send/dispatch results report turn-start proof (native-turn-start-confirmed) separately from message persistence. A delivery with no turn start within a bounded window escalates to host recovery (P-004/P-005 items above) and is reported as not-picked-up. First verify the peer claim [peer:su-c210fe1b] that wake-executor.ts degradeToInboxOrDrop reports 'delivered' on inbox fallback. Measured: loop fires to stalled sessions are all status=delivered (inbox / psu-socket-inject / suppressed-redundant) with no turn. importance: high — note: WI-10008157 done 22:17:40Z: turn_proof ledger dimension (migration 1441) + pickup sweep + D-005 enqueue semantics; live-verified after 1441 applied; spec AUTO-BAR-R-6-P-006@2 PASS.\n- **P-007** `done` stalled-loops-guard repairs instead of disarming. On a turns-stalled verdict, read host health (auth error, unbound respawn, pending duplicate deliveries) and route to the recovery items; disarm only when the host is healthy and turns are genuinely not wanted. Never leave a claim-holding AUTO session with zero wake sources: fall back to a bounded await plus a fleet-leader/owner alert. Reuse silent-halt-reconcile.ts; coordinate with the WI-37546 false-positive reviver (it only revives after new tool activity, which a wedged session cannot produce). Measured: 61 turns-stalled disarms; 37 dead-auth; 57 now hold claims with no wake. importance: high blocked-by: P-004, P-005 — note: ← WI-10008158 completed (done)\n- **P-008** `done` Fix the instructions that teach premature stops. Follow the su-persona-render-and-edit-path runbook; update loop.test.ts / end.test.ts / agent-launch-core.test.ts; run the su llm-test. importance: high — note: ← WI-10008159 completed (done)\n- **P-009** `done` Make the wake-less guard real. loop:end refuses an AUTO session that still holds unfinished claims and has no non-inbox wake source, unless the goal is done / claims released, an owner stop directive exists, or a typed fleet wind-down cue applies. A claim with a recorded external blocker (work_items:set_blocker) no longer counts against the 2-claim cap (or auto-parks on the blocker with a resume watch), so an agent holding two blocked items can pull independent work instead of parking. Measured: 16 parked agents cite the 2/2 claim cap. — note: WI-10008161 closed done: adequacy pass, mutation caught (21025441), certify card EI-25650609330847283.\n- **P-011** `done` Detector and honest liveness. (a) The presence oracle reports a session that cannot take a turn (dead auth, unbound respawn, quota/403/model refusal) as wedged/suspect, not 'parked'. (b) A standing alarm (existing condition-reconciler / infra-liveness surface) fires when wedged sessions, or parked AUTO sessions holding claims with no wake source, exceed a threshold, with per-class counts visible on /admin.
